Setta frontend third-party notices

This inventory includes the installed browser dependency graph of all four
apps, including optional and tree-shaken code that may not reach a browser.
The separately installed basedpyright language server and its dependencies
retain their own distribution notices.
Regenerate with: python packages/setta-runtime/scripts/frontend_notices.py --write
